### New River Pharmaceuticals Inc. (NRPH) — Shire plc

- **Announced / Closed:** 2007-02-20 / 2007-04-19 (completed)
- **Deal size:** 2,371.3 $M | **Final offer:** $64.0/sh | **Payment:** cash
- **Premium:** +10% — vs. last closing price of $58.35 on 2007-02-16 (pre-announcement Friday)
- **Company stage:** approved+marketed | **Lead asset:** VYVANSE (lisdexamfetamine dimesylate) (Approved (FDA approval Feb 23, 2007))
- **Offers:** low $61.5 → high $64.0 over 3 round(s)
- **Parties interested:** 15 | **Process:** broad auction | **Direction:** mixed
- **Engagement→signing:** 48 days (2007-01-10 → 2007-02-20)
- **Leaked ≥1mo early:** Yes — Rumors of Shire as a likely acquirer surfaced by January 13, 2007, and heavy pre-announcement speculation on February 16, 2007, indicated imminent deal.
- **Prior price moves:** 3mo +5% | 6mo +122% | 12mo +140% | 2yr +386%
- **Value-inflection events:**
    - 2006-10-13: Unidentified trigger event; stock price surged 74.8% from prior week's close of $26.21 to $45.82. Speculation likely related to positive clinical/regulatory news for VYVANSE given subsequent high volume. (+75%)
- **Note:** Deal was driven by Shire's desire to consolidate VYVANSE ownership post-FDA approval. Despite a broad process contacting 14 other parties, no superior bids emerged. The final $64.00 price was a modest 9.7% premium over the pre-announcement close ($58.35), though the stock had already surged significantly in the prior months due to commercial prospects.
